# 1507  algorithm   ⟦0⟧=`l`  ⟦1⟧=`j`  ⟦2⟧=`cnt`  ⟦3⟧=`nums[j] < nums[l]`  ⟦4⟧=`dp[j]`  ⟦5⟧=`nums[j] > nums[l]`  ⟦6⟧=O(n²)  ⟦7⟧=O(n)

Odd length wale har run ke liye, answer ko ⟦0⟧/2 se multiply karta hai; even-length runs ek factor of 1 contribute karti hain. Final product return kiya jata hai.

# 1966  algorithm   ⟦0⟧=`A`  ⟦1⟧=`B`
EN  Insert each child of every parent from ⟦0⟧ into a stack keyed by the parent. Then iterate the edges of ⟦1⟧; for each edge, the expected mirrored child must be at the top of the corresponding stack. If all edges match, the trees are mirrors.
RU  ⟦0⟧ ke har parent ke har child ko parent se keyed ek stack mein insert karta hai. Phir ⟦1⟧ ke edges par iterate karta hai; har edge ke liye, expected mirrored child corresponding stack ke top par hona chahiye. Agar sab edges match karein, trees mirrors hain.

RU  Agar N 2 se kam ho, N ⟦0⟧ return karta hai. Warna recursively ⟦1⟧ aur ⟦2⟧ compute karke unka sum leta hai. Ye bare N ke liye exponential time aur deep recursion deta hai.

# 1973  algorithm
EN  Iterate through the input digits, building combinations incrementally. For the first digit, create one‑character strings. For subsequent digits, extend each existing string with every possible character for the current digit, handling repeated digits by reusing the previously selected character. After processing each digit, swap the temporary results into the main output list.
RU  Input digits ke through iterate karta hai, combinations incrementally build karte hue. Pehle digit ke liye, one-character strings create karta hai. Baad ke digits ke liye, har existing string ko current digit ke har possible character se extend karta hai, repeated digits ko previously selected character reuse karke handle karte hue. Har digit process hone ke baad, temporary results ko main output list mein swap kar deta hai.

Phir ye current middle member ke sath ban sakne wali valid teams ki tadaad calculate karta hai aur use total count mein add kar deta hai.

RU  ⟦0⟧ ek sliding window ke sath string scan karta hai, '1's ke har maximal run ke liye n·(n+1)/2 sum karte hue. flip har bit invert karta hai. ⟦1⟧ original aur flipped strings par ⟦0⟧ ko reuse karta hai, dono results add karte hue.
